__________________ test_xss_exact_unit[extra-mb_w96_mid_row] ___________________

kind = 'extra', case = ('mb_w96_mid_row', 128, 96, 64, 64, 1, ...), xchg = True
device = MeshDevice(1x1 grid, 1 devices)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind,case,xchg", _UNIT, ids=_UNIT_IDS)
    def test_xss_exact_unit(kind, case, xchg, device):
        import torch
        import ttnn

        _, H, W, c_in, c_out, d, act, wdt, fidelity, relu, cores = case
        DT = {"bf8": ttnn.bfloat8_b, "bf16": ttnn.bfloat16}
        dtype, weight_dtype = DT[act], DT[wdt]
        grid = device.compute_with_storage_grid_size()
        m_tiles = H * W // 32
        per_core = -(-m_tiles // cores)
        cores = -(-m_tiles // per_core)
        if cores > grid.x * grid.y:
            pytest.skip("grid")
        mc = ttnn.MemoryConfig(
            ttnn.TensorMemoryLayout.HEIGHT_SHARDED,
            ttnn.BufferType.L1,
            ttnn.ShardSpec(
                ttnn.num_cores_to_corerangeset(cores, grid, row_wise=True),
                [per_core * 32, c_in],
                ttnn.ShardOrientation.ROW_MAJOR,
            ),
        )
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        tx = torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tp = (torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in) * 64).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tw = (torch.randn(c_out, c_in, 3, 3) / (3 * c_in**0.5)).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tb = torch.randn(c_out).to(torch.bfloat16)
        prep = dict(
            stride=1,
            padding=d,
            dilation=d,
            input_dtype=dtype,
            input_layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,
            weight_dtype=weight_dtype,
            input_memory_config=mc,
            device=device,
        )
        conv = dict(
            input_height=H,
            input_width=W,
            padding=d,
            stride=1,
            dilation=d,
            activation="relu" if relu else None,
            compute_kernel_config=ttnn.init_device_compute_kernel_config(
                device.arch(), math_fidelity=getattr(ttnn.MathFidelity, fidelity), fp32_dest_acc_en=True
            ),
        )
        x = tx.float().reshape(1, H, W, c_in).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        ref = torch.nn.functional.conv2d(x, tw.float(), tb.float(), padding=d, dilation=d)
        if relu:
            ref = torch.relu(ref)
        ref = ref.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(1, 1, -1, c_out)
>       outs, plans = _run_three(
            lambda poison: ttnn.from_torch(
                tp if poison else tx, dtype=dtype, layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT, device=device, memory_config=mc
            ),
            conv,
            prep,
            tw,
            tb,
            force=xchg,
        )

kind = 'extra', case = ('mb_ragged_w64', 96, 64, 64, 64, 1, ...), xchg = True
device = MeshDevice(1x1 grid, 1 devices)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind,case,xchg", _UNIT, ids=_UNIT_IDS)
    def test_xss_exact_unit(kind, case, xchg, device):
        import torch
        import ttnn

        _, H, W, c_in, c_out, d, act, wdt, fidelity, relu, cores = case
        DT = {"bf8": ttnn.bfloat8_b, "bf16": ttnn.bfloat16}
        dtype, weight_dtype = DT[act], DT[wdt]
        grid = device.compute_with_storage_grid_size()
        m_tiles = H * W // 32
        per_core = -(-m_tiles // cores)
        cores = -(-m_tiles // per_core)
        if cores > grid.x * grid.y:
            pytest.skip("grid")
        mc = ttnn.MemoryConfig(
            ttnn.TensorMemoryLayout.HEIGHT_SHARDED,
            ttnn.BufferType.L1,
            ttnn.ShardSpec(
                ttnn.num_cores_to_corerangeset(cores, grid, row_wise=True),
                [per_core * 32, c_in],
                ttnn.ShardOrientation.ROW_MAJOR,
            ),
        )
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        tx = torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tp = (torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in) * 64).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tw = (torch.randn(c_out, c_in, 3, 3) / (3 * c_in**0.5)).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tb = torch.randn(c_out).to(torch.bfloat16)
        prep = dict(
            stride=1,
            padding=d,
            dilation=d,
            input_dtype=dtype,
            input_layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,
            weight_dtype=weight_dtype,
            input_memory_config=mc,
            device=device,
        )
        conv = dict(
            input_height=H,
            input_width=W,
            padding=d,
            stride=1,
            dilation=d,
            activation="relu" if relu else None,
            compute_kernel_config=ttnn.init_device_compute_kernel_config(
                device.arch(), math_fidelity=getattr(ttnn.MathFidelity, fidelity), fp32_dest_acc_en=True
            ),
        )
        x = tx.float().reshape(1, H, W, c_in).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        ref = torch.nn.functional.conv2d(x, tw.float(), tb.float(), padding=d, dilation=d)
        if relu:
            ref = torch.relu(ref)
        ref = ref.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(1, 1, -1, c_out)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind,case,xchg", _UNIT, ids=_UNIT_IDS)
    def test_xss_exact_unit(kind, case, xchg, device):
        import torch
        import ttnn

        _, H, W, c_in, c_out, d, act, wdt, fidelity, relu, cores = case
        DT = {"bf8": ttnn.bfloat8_b, "bf16": ttnn.bfloat16}
        dtype, weight_dtype = DT[act], DT[wdt]
        grid = device.compute_with_storage_grid_size()
        m_tiles = H * W // 32
        per_core = -(-m_tiles // cores)
        cores = -(-m_tiles // per_core)
        if cores > grid.x * grid.y:
            pytest.skip("grid")
        mc = ttnn.MemoryConfig(
            ttnn.TensorMemoryLayout.HEIGHT_SHARDED,
            ttnn.BufferType.L1,
            ttnn.ShardSpec(
                ttnn.num_cores_to_corerangeset(cores, grid, row_wise=True),
                [per_core * 32, c_in],
                ttnn.ShardOrientation.ROW_MAJOR,
            ),
        )
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        tx = torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tp = (torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in) * 64).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tw = (torch.randn(c_out, c_in, 3, 3) / (3 * c_in**0.5)).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tb = torch.randn(c_out).to(torch.bfloat16)
        prep = dict(
            stride=1,
            padding=d,
            dilation=d,
            input_dtype=dtype,
            input_layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,
            weight_dtype=weight_dtype,
            input_memory_config=mc,
            device=device,
        )
        conv = dict(
            input_height=H,
            input_width=W,
            padding=d,
            stride=1,
            dilation=d,
            activation="relu" if relu else None,
            compute_kernel_config=ttnn.init_device_compute_kernel_config(
                device.arch(), math_fidelity=getattr(ttnn.MathFidelity, fidelity), fp32_dest_acc_en=True
            ),
        )
        x = tx.float().reshape(1, H, W, c_in).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        ref = torch.nn.functional.conv2d(x, tw.float(), tb.float(), padding=d, dilation=d)
        if relu:
            ref = torch.relu(ref)
        ref = ref.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(1, 1, -1, c_out)

kind = 'extra', case = ('n2_d3', 48, 96, 64, 64, 3, ...), xchg = True

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind,case,xchg", _UNIT, ids=_UNIT_IDS)
    def test_xss_exact_unit(kind, case, xchg, device):
        import torch
        import ttnn

        _, H, W, c_in, c_out, d, act, wdt, fidelity, relu, cores = case
        DT = {"bf8": ttnn.bfloat8_b, "bf16": ttnn.bfloat16}
        dtype, weight_dtype = DT[act], DT[wdt]
        grid = device.compute_with_storage_grid_size()
        m_tiles = H * W // 32
        per_core = -(-m_tiles // cores)
        cores = -(-m_tiles // per_core)
        if cores > grid.x * grid.y:
            pytest.skip("grid")
        mc = ttnn.MemoryConfig(
            ttnn.TensorMemoryLayout.HEIGHT_SHARDED,
            ttnn.BufferType.L1,
            ttnn.ShardSpec(
                ttnn.num_cores_to_corerangeset(cores, grid, row_wise=True),
                [per_core * 32, c_in],
                ttnn.ShardOrientation.ROW_MAJOR,
            ),
        )
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        tx = torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tp = (torch.randn(1, 1, H * W, c_in) * 64).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tw = (torch.randn(c_out, c_in, 3, 3) / (3 * c_in**0.5)).to(torch.bfloat16)
        tb = torch.randn(c_out).to(torch.bfloat16)
        prep = dict(
            stride=1,
            padding=d,
            dilation=d,
            input_dtype=dtype,
            input_layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,
            weight_dtype=weight_dtype,
            input_memory_config=mc,
            device=device,
        )
        conv = dict(
            input_height=H,
            input_width=W,
            padding=d,
            stride=1,
            dilation=d,
            activation="relu" if relu else None,
            compute_kernel_config=ttnn.init_device_compute_kernel_config(
                device.arch(), math_fidelity=getattr(ttnn.MathFidelity, fidelity), fp32_dest_acc_en=True
            ),
        )
        x = tx.float().reshape(1, H, W, c_in).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        ref = torch.nn.functional.conv2d(x, tw.float(), tb.float(), padding=d, dilation=d)
        if relu:
            ref = torch.relu(ref)
        ref = ref.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(1, 1, -1, c_out)

            input_memory_config=mc,
            device=device,
        )
        conv = dict(
            input_height=H,
            input_width=W,
            padding=d,
            stride=1,
            dilation=d,
            activation="relu" if relu else None,
            compute_kernel_config=ttnn.init_device_compute_kernel_config(
                device.arch(), math_fidelity=getattr(ttnn.MathFidelity, fidelity), fp32_dest_acc_en=True
            ),
        )
        x = tx.float().reshape(1, H, W, c_in).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        ref = torch.nn.functional.conv2d(x, tw.float(), tb.float(), padding=d, dilation=d)
        if relu:
            ref = torch.relu(ref)
        ref = ref.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(1, 1, -1, c_out)
>       outs, plans = _run_three(
            lambda poison: ttnn.from_torch(
                tp if poison else tx, dtype=dtype, layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT, device=device, memory_config=mc
            ),
            conv,
            prep,
            tw,
            tb,
            force=xchg,
        )
